## The no-login launcher variant (14 Oct, 18:21 EDT; in its own commit, for its own independent review)

**Supplied at run time, not baked in.** The launcher program reaches the container in the exec's argv, as `docker exec ... /usr/bin/python3 -I -S -c LAUNCHER_SOURCE ...` (`run_take.launcher_argv`, `exec_argv`).
The image provides only `/usr/bin/python3`:
- `box/Dockerfile` installs python3 and copies no launcher;
- `box/image.lock` and `box/image_lock.py` hold no launcher or `run_take` entry (grep for "launcher" and "run_take" finds nothing).

So no image lock changes, and the variant was built, per glitch-06's ruling.

**The change** (`box/run_take.py`):
- `LAUNCHER_SOURCE`'s one credential line `e[k]=os.environ[k]` becomes `if k!='-':e[k]=os.environ[k]`.
- `NO_LOGIN = "-"` is the reserved name. No variable NAME can match it (`_NAME`).
- `launcher_argv(login, values, inner, *, harness=None)` admits NO_LOGIN only for a Codex row (`harness == CODEX`), and only through `codex_lease_gate()`, which is closed: NotReady(CODEX_SUBSCRIPTION_NOT_READY). Any other harness is refused with "the no-login launcher name is admitted only for a Codex row".
- Every other road, Claude Code's included, still copies its one credential by name.

**Test** (`TestNoLoginLauncher`):
- The launcher program runs in-process with `os.execvpe` faked, so no process starts.
- Claude Code's credential is still copied.
- With NO_LOGIN nothing is copied, even when the exec's environment holds a planted `CODEX_API_KEY` or `OPENAI_API_KEY`.
- The closed gate refuses NO_LOGIN; with the gate open, the argv is exact.
- Claude Code, Gemini and no harness are each refused by rule.

**Red first** (`runs/116`, against `6c21320b`): no `NO_LOGIN`.

**Mutants** (`mutants/no_login_launcher_mutants.py`, `runs/117`): 5 of 5 red, none alive.
- The launcher copies the reserved name.
- The launcher skips every credential.
- No gate on the reserved name.
- The reserved name is admitted for any harness.
- The reserved name is not reserved.

**Runner counts** (`runs/118-*`, Mac, light gate passed before each module, one at a time): all OK.

| Module | Tests |
|---|---|
| test_run_take | 52 |
| test_env_allowlist | 28 |
| test_stub_take | 19 |
| test_preflight | 57 |
| test_live_box | 38 |
| test_box_integration | 61 |

Nothing builds on the launcher variant until its review passes. The Codex row change after this commit uses only the `NO_LOGIN` constant, as Codex's "no environment login" marker. It does not use the launcher's behaviour.

## The Codex row: subscriptions only, no environment login, named route variables (14 Oct, 18:28 EDT)

**Ruling 1 (glitch-06): the Codex row.**
- `TAKE_ENV[CODEX]` no longer carries `CODEX_API_KEY`. No API-key route exists, ever (the 13 Oct amendment).
- `LOGIN_VARIABLE[CODEX]` is `NO_LOGIN` (`"-"`), meaning "no environment login": the lease road. It is set beside `NO_LOGIN`'s definition. If the Codex row ever carries an environment credential again, the module refuses to load.
- `CODEX_API_KEY` used to be subtracted from a Codex take's refused set as "its own login". Both it and `OPENAI_API_KEY` are now refused for a Codex take, in the caller's environment and in any exec request, by name only.

**Explicit refusals, never a KeyError, in this lane's modules:**
- `run_take.read_login(path, NO_LOGIN)` refuses with "a Codex row has no environment login: its login is the lease (place_lease)".
- `scan_record.read_login(path, "-", ...)` refuses with "...its login is the lease (read_lease_tokens)", before any other rule, whatever the allowed set holds.
- `exec_argv` still refuses Codex outright (NotReady).
- **Preflight** refuses a Codex take whose create-time environment carries `CODEX_API_KEY` or `OPENAI_API_KEY` (check `no-container-socket`, naming the variable, never the value).
  - That refusal already held before this change, through VENDOR_KEY_VARIABLES. The new test pins it for a Codex world.

**Ruling 3 (glitch-06): `VENDOR_BILLING_ROUTE_VARIABLES[CODEX]`, named only from the pinned binary's own strings.**
Source: `lane-reports/live-box-evidence-2026-10-13/codex-auth-binary-markers.json`, key `sources[0].matches[N].context`, for the binary `.../codex-linux-x64/vendor/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/bin/codex`.
The binary's strings run together with no separators, so each name ends at the next `CODEX_`/`OPENAI_` prefix or at a lower-case byte.
These are refused if present, for every arm, like the other vendors' names.

| Variable | Evidence (`matches[N]`) | What sits beside it |
|---|---|---|
| `CODEX_AUTHAPI_BASE_URL` | 46, 50 | `https://auth.openai.com/api/accounts` |
| `CODEX_AGENT_IDENTITY_AUTHAPI_BASE_URL` | 68 | |
| `CODEX_AGENT_IDENTITY_JWKS_BASE_URL` | 46, 68 | |
| `CODEX_REVOKE_TOKEN_URL_OVERRIDE` | 65 | `https://auth.openai.com/oauth/revoke` |
| `CODEX_CONNECTORS_TOKEN` | 33 | |
| `OPENAI_ORGANIZATION` | 40 | the `OpenAI-Organization` header |
| `OPENAI_FEDERATION_RULE_ID` | 31 | |
| `OPENAI_IDENTITY_TOKEN_FILE` | 31 | |

`CODEX_API_KEY` and `OPENAI_API_KEY` (matches 66 and 79, beside "API key login is required") were already in `VENDOR_KEY_VARIABLES`.

## The pinned Codex binary's environment-name sweep (14 Oct, 19:11 EDT)

glitch-06 checked athena's environment, by name only: none of `AWS_*`, `OPENAI_*` or `CODEX_*` is set in the login shell or the user manager.

**Gate.** `athena_suite.py list` was read before each step. Only the non-solo `gap4-harness-alone-1f03258` (jobs=12) was in progress, and no process carried `--solo`. The integration lane's SOLO battery had not started.

**The sweep** (on athena, as javier; `probes/codex_env_name_sweep.py`):
- The binary was copied out of the pinned take-o image (`box/codex/image.lock` image_id `sha256:21b214159e…`) with `docker create --pull=never --network none` and `docker cp`. It was never run, and the container was removed (0 left for that image).
- The binary is `.../codex-linux-x64/vendor/x86_64-unknown-linux-musl/bin/codex`: 294,415,048 bytes, sha256 `628e35487d888a28f0427fc37501da529dd3d81fcbad7a2c3421c1e8670d92e1`. That is the same binary the markers file cites.
- athena has no `strings`, so the probe extracts printable runs of 4+ bytes in Python, the `strings -a -n 4` rule. It then applies the regex `(CODEX|OPENAI|AWS|AZURE|ANTHROPIC|GOOGLE)_[A-Z0-9_]+`, recording the byte after each match and two short contexts.
- That found 173 raw matches. The temp folder `/home/javier/gap4-tools/tmp-gap4box` (the binary included) was deleted and checked absent.
- **Record:** `probes/codex-env-name-sweep-athena-20261014.json`, sha256 `ddb31eec8b337c0614ef82895d5be04662e6a63a558cc42d3e6163a8e90484bd`.

**The classification** (`probes/classify_codex_env_names.py`).
- It is deterministic, from the committed record only. Any name its table does not place stops it, so it covers the sweep whole.
- Boundaries, as before:
  - Each raw match is split at every later known prefix.
  - Explicit `GLUED` entries map matches that run into a following word or a non-prefixed variable. For example, `AWS_DEFAULT_REGIONAmazon` gives `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`, and `CODEX_CA_CERTIFICATESSL_CERT_FILE…` gives `CODEX_CA_CERTIFICATE`.
  - For a refused name, the one-capital variant is refused too (as `ISSUERA` was).
- **Record:** `probes/codex-env-name-classification-20261014.json`, sha256 `ed55ee5a1f983512ef044396fc0390146fb3940a5c1729542c8fb4c8b159fb4f`.
- **Counts:** 195 split names, of which 37 are credentials, 50 routes, 16 ambiguous, 63 harmless and 29 fragments. That gives 95 names to refuse if present.
  - **Credentials:** AWS keys, tokens, profiles and roles; Azure and Google credential variables; `CODEX_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `CODEX_CONNECTORS_TOKEN`, `CODEX_GITHUB_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `CODEX_GUARDIAN_DECISIONS_API_KEY`; the exec-server noise auth token and account id; the OpenAI workload-identity variables; the app-server login client and issuer.
  - **Routes** (billing, endpoint, proxy, trust-store or sandbox switches): every `*_BASE_URL` and `*_URL_OVERRIDE`; the exec-server and cloud-tasks endpoints; the originator override; `CODEX_CA_CERTIFICATE`; the network-proxy and sandbox switches; `OPENAI_ORGANIZATION` and `OPENAI_CLUSTER`; the AWS region, endpoint, IMDS and signing settings.
  - **Ambiguous, refused anyway** (refusing more fails closed): `CODEX_AUTH` (a keyring secret name), `CODEX_PERMISSION`, the daemon telemetry handoff, the remote-control switch, `AWS_SIGV4`, the network-policy and proxy-snapshot names, and the shell-snapshot proxy and override names.
    - Two of these, `CODEX_NETWORK_PROXY_SNAPSHOT_BROKERED_UNSET_` and `…_VALUE_`, are name prefixes. Refusing the exact strings does not refuse names that extend them. Noted as a residual.
  - **Harmless:**
    - `CODEX_HOME`, which is the Codex row's own config home;
    - TUI, install, managed-by, version and session ids;
    - the shell-snapshot internals;
    - the AWS retry, checksum and compression settings.
  - **Fragments:** machine-code or truncated bytes such as `OPENAI_BM1` and `CODEX_THH`.
  - No `ANTHROPIC_` name is in the binary.

**The change** (`box/run_take.py`):
- `CODEX_BINARY_SWEEP_REFUSED` holds the 95 names, in the classification's order.
- Its names not already in `VENDOR_BILLING_ROUTE_VARIABLES[CODEX]` join that entry, so every arm refuses them if present.
- `TestCodexBinarySweep` binds it:
  - the classification carries the committed sweep's sha256, and the binary sha256 is `628e3548…`;
  - it re-derives the split names from the sweep and requires a row for each;
  - the refuse list equals every credential, route and ambiguous row;
  - `run_take`'s tuple equals the file;
  - every arm refuses every name, no arm's own row name is refused, and `CODEX_HOME` is not refused.

**Red first** (`runs/131`, against `06775d56`): `TestCodexBinarySweep` (no tuple), `TestCodexRow` and `test_refused_sets` each fail.

**Mutants** (`mutants/codex_row_mutants.py`, `runs/132`): 12 of 12 red, none alive. Three are new: a sweep name dropped, the sweep not merged into the Codex entry, and `CODEX_HOME` refused with the sweep.
- `OPENAI_API_KEY` is now refused twice: in `VENDOR_KEY_VARIABLES` and in the sweep. So its mutant removes it from both, and it is red.

## The sweep's prefix residual refused (14 Oct, 19:31 EDT)

**glitch-06's ruling:** any environment name starting with `CODEX_NETWORK_PROXY_SNAPSHOT_BROKERED_UNSET_` or `CODEX_NETWORK_PROXY_SNAPSHOT_BROKERED_VALUE_` is refused for every arm. Refusing more fails closed.

The row allowlist already keeps any such name out of the agent's environment.

## The pinned Gemini CLI's environment-name sweep, and its list filled (14 Oct, 19:43 EDT)

**Gate.** `athena_suite.py list` was read first. Only the non-solo `gap4-harness-alone-1f03258` was in progress, and no process carried `--solo`.

**The sweep** (on athena, as javier; `probes/gemini_env_name_sweep.py`):
- **Source:** the package `/usr/local/lib/node_modules/@google/gemini-cli` (version 0.64.0), copied out of the pinned take-g image (`box/gemini/image.lock` image_id `sha256:d10aa00b…`) with `docker create --pull=never --network none` and `docker cp`.
- It was never run. The container was removed (0 left), and the temp folder was deleted and checked absent.
- **What was read:** all 447 text files, dependencies included (the CLI ships them bundled). Package text digest `6d803815…`.
- **Three kinds of match:**
  - `process.env.X`;
  - `env["X"]` / `env['X']`;
  - the vendor-name regex: `GEMINI_`, `GOOGLE_`, `CLOUDSDK_`, `GCP_`, `GCLOUD_`, `CLOUD_ML_`, any name containing `VERTEX`, and the other vendors' prefixes.
- **Result:** 519 names. Of these, 257 are read from the environment, and 262 are only string constants (telemetry attribute keys, header names, defaults).
- **Record:** `probes/gemini-env-name-sweep-athena-20261014.json`, sha256 `fc80791de007ce9bd4d85419369e659e16a8bc674964a9b24c5d62e8672e9f96`.

**The classification** (`probes/classify_gemini_env_names.py`; record `probes/gemini-env-name-classification-20261014.json`, sha256 `5be2eef94ff23881bedca53e4385736a172fd91b42e784480bf453b1ed24b075`):
- **How it places names:**
  - Every name the CLI reads from the environment must be in the explicit table, or the run stops.
  - A name found only as a constant is refused as ambiguous if it carries a risky word (auth, token, key, credential, billing, URL, endpoint, proxy, project, location, region, account, Vertex, sandbox, model, header, certificate, telemetry, storage, settings, path, file, config, metadata and others). Otherwise it is a harmless constant.
- **Counts:**
  - 5 row: `HOME`, `PATH`, `LANG`, `HTTPS_PROXY`, `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`.
  - 1 own login: `GEMINI_API_KEY`.
  - 13 credential.
  - 60 route.
  - 135 ambiguous.
  - 305 harmless.
  - That gives **208 names to refuse if present.**
- **What is refused:**
  - **Credentials:** the AWS keys, `GOOGLE_API_KEY`, `GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS` (both cases), `GOOGLE_CLOUD_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `GITHUB_TOKEN`, `CRUX_API_KEY`, `DOTENV_KEY`, the IDE auth token, OTLP headers and custom headers.
  - **Routes:**
    - project and quota;
    - location, region and universe domain;
    - the Gemini and Vertex base URLs and the Code Assist endpoint;
    - the Vertex, GCA and compute-ADC switches;
    - the auth type, mechanism and override;
    - `GEMINI_MODEL`;
    - every sandbox switch;
    - `HTTP_PROXY`, `NO_PROXY` and the lower-case proxies;
    - the metadata server;
    - the system-settings and system-prompt overrides;
    - the IDE server;
    - the OAuth callback;
    - the telemetry exporters.
  - **Ambiguous:** among others, `NODE_OPTIONS`, `LOG_TOKENS`, `DEBUG_AUTH`, the GCP runtime-detection names (`K_SERVICE`, `GAE_*`, `FUNCTION_*`), and the trust and workspace settings.
- **Not refused:** `GEMINI_API_KEY` stays the Gemini arm's login, refused only for the other arms. No take row's own name is refused for any arm.

**The fill** (`box/run_take.py`):
- `GEMINI_PACKAGE_SWEEP_REFUSED` holds the 208 names.
- `VENDOR_BILLING_ROUTE_VARIABLES[GEMINI]` was PENDING and is now that tuple, so every arm refuses them.
- `run_take.pending()` now lists only `TakeSpec.resources`.

**Consequences:**
- `take_environment` no longer refuses Gemini. **A Gemini exec is no longer stopped by the PENDING list.** It still has to pass every other check: the row, the model pin, the login file and the refused names.
- **Host environments:** the refused names are checked in the host process's own environment for every arm. So a Mac or athena shell with any of them set refuses every take until it is unset. That includes `HTTP_PROXY`, `NO_PROXY`, `NODE_OPTIONS`, `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT` and the AWS names.
  - This Mac's shell sets none of the 208, checked by name.
  - athena's driver environment has not been checked against this list.

**Tests:**
- New: `TestGeminiPackageSweep`, the same bindings as the Codex sweep:
  - the sweep's sha256, and the pinned version read from `box/gemini/image.lock`;
  - every name placed, and the refuse list equal to every credential, route and ambiguous row;
  - `run_take`'s tuple equal to the file;
  - every arm refuses every name, no row name is refused, `GEMINI_API_KEY` stays Gemini's own, and `take_environment` admits Gemini.
- Rebound to the fill:
  - `TestExecArgv`: a Gemini exec with its list put back to PENDING is still NotReady.
  - `TestPendingAndNames`: its mutant is now "billing-back-to-pending".
  - `test_refused_sets`: its closed set adds both sweeps. The "vendor-list-short" mutant now also takes `OPENAI_API_KEY` and `GOOGLE_API_KEY` out of the sweep lists, and is red on exactly those two.
  - test_preflight's `EXPECT_RT`, and test_env_allowlist's E5.pending (red-tested with the entry put back).

**Red first** (`runs/138`, against `8a03e69d`): no `GEMINI_PACKAGE_SWEEP_REFUSED`.

**Mutants** (`mutants/gemini_sweep_mutants.py`, `runs/139`): 5 of 5 red, none alive:
- the entry back to PENDING;
- a name dropped;
- `GEMINI_API_KEY` refused for Gemini;
- `GEMINI_CLI_HOME` refused;
- `HTTPS_PROXY` refused.

**Runner counts** (Mac, light gate passed before each module, one at a time):
- **`runs/141`** (the three rebound modules): test_run_take 57, test_env_allowlist 28, test_preflight 58, all OK.
- **`runs/140`**, all OK: test_stub_take 19, test_live_box 38, test_claude_code_adapter 19, test_gemini_adapter 138, test_wave4_bindings 13, test_take_config 9, test_drive_commit 31, test_box_integration 61.
- **`runs/140`**, baselines unchanged: test_codex_adapter 13 failures (the same 13 as `runs/122`), test_walk_builders 3, test_walk_runner 46.

## The review of 06775d56^..bea73659: CHANGES (one MAJOR), and the folds (14 Oct, 19:55 EDT)

`08d32c26`, the create-time prefix change, was already committed when the review ran. The reviewer saw it uncommitted.

**MAJOR: the classification files were bound to nothing.** The reviewer reproduced it, and so did I (`runs/146`, the mutants against `0ee767a3` as committed).
Relabelling `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT` as harmless, and dropping it from both the classification and `GEMINI_PACKAGE_SWEEP_REFUSED`, left test_run_take green. The same held for a Codex route.

**The fix:**
- `TestCodexBinarySweep` and `TestGeminiPackageSweep` now run the committed classifier on the committed sweep and require its output to equal the committed classification's bytes.
- The classifier runs in-process (`run_classifier`, via `runpy`), because test_run_take's process guard forbids starting a process.
- With the bindings, all three relabel mutants are red (`runs/147`).

**MINOR 2: each sweep is bound to its lock.**
- `sweep["image"]` must equal `box/<arm>/image.lock`'s `image_id`, for both arms.
- The Codex binary's full sha256 must equal the sweep's, the classification's and the markers file's (`sources[0].sha256`): `628e35487d888a28f0427fc37501da529dd3d81fcbad7a2c3421c1e8670d92e1`.
- Two mutants are red: the Codex sweep bound to another image, and a binary sha256 that differs only after its first 16 characters.

**MINOR 3: the glued non-prefixed names are now rows of their own** (the Codex classifier's `GLUED_TAILS`, with their classes in `TAIL_CLASS`):
- **Route, refused for every arm:**
  - trust-store names: `SSL_CERT_FILE`, `SSL_CERT_DIR`, `REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE`, `CURL_CA_BUNDLE`, `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`, `GIT_SSL_CAINFO`, `CARGO_HTTP_CAINFO`, `BUNDLE_SSL_CA_CERT`;
  - proxy names: `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY`, `ELECTRON_GET_USE_PROXY`, `HTTP_PROXY`.
- **Credential:** `NODE_REPL_AUTH_TOKEN`.
- **Ambiguous:** `EXEC_WRAPPER`, and `REQUEST_DISPATCH_MODE` (whose end is cut by the next glued byte).
- **Row, not refused:** `HTTPS_PROXY`. It is every take row's egress proxy (`TAKE_ENV` sets it to the proxy for each arm), so it stays a row name for every row.
- **Harmless:** `PWD`, `OLDPWD`.
- That adds 14 refused names, so `CODEX_BINARY_SWEEP_REFUSED` now holds 109.
- **athena rechecked** by name only, against all refused names from both sweeps and both prefixes. Its ssh login shell plus the user manager carry 23 names (glitch-06 counted 21, I think without the shell's own `SHLVL` and `_`). There were 0 hits. This Mac's shell also has 0 hits.

**MINOR 4:** `WEB_HOST` and `EDITOR_URL` move to ambiguous, so they are refused.

**NIT 5:** the Gemini classifier's risky words gain `SECRET`, `PASS`, `HOST`, `PORT`, `ROUTING` and `API_DEFAULT`.
- The rerun: 5 row, 1 own login, 13 credential, 60 route, 146 ambiguous, 294 harmless. That is **219 names to refuse**, up from 208.
- `GEMINI_PACKAGE_SWEEP_REFUSED` follows the file.

**The new shas, cited everywhere the files are:**
- `codex-env-name-classification-20261014.json`: `c0cbb46d0459eab132b6645d21e592f883764a43aa5862b6cb24d4d0db444c18` (was `ed55ee5a…`).
- `gemini-env-name-classification-20261014.json`: `0a364720d241526c4bc4088fede2155b628856ed014aa4c8a194f9a3bcc2e754` (was `5be2eef9…`).
- `classify_codex_env_names.py`: `671fa4e5…`. `classify_gemini_env_names.py`: `59002057…`.
- The two sweep records are unchanged: `ddb31eec…` and `fc80791d…`.
- `run_take.py`'s citations carry the new classification shas. The tests bind by bytes, not by a cited sha.

**NIT 6: the Gemini sweep's coverage residual.** The sweep finds names written out in the package text. It does not find:
- **Destructured reads**, such as `const { FOO } = process.env`, unless the name also appears in another matched form. The vendor regex still catches vendor-prefixed names written anywhere.
- **Dynamic reads**: names built at run time (`process.env["GEMINI_" + x]`, template strings) or taken from a settings file.
- **Names Node itself reads:** `NODE_OPTIONS` appears in the Gemini package text and is refused. `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` is refused through the Codex sweep. Others never appear in the package text, such as `NODE_TLS_REJECT_UNAUTHORIZED`, `UV_THREADPOOL_SIZE` and the OpenSSL variables.
- **What bounds it:** the take row allowlist starts the agent from an empty environment with exactly its row, so an unlisted name cannot reach the agent from the host. The refusal lists are defence on the host side.

**Mutants:**
- `mutants/sweep_binding_mutants.py` (`runs/147`): 5 of 5 red, none alive. It changes a committed record through a patched `open()` and patches `run_take`'s tuples to match.
- The earlier sets, rerun (`runs/148`): Codex row 12, Gemini sweep 5, prefixes 4, all red.
- The Codex-row mutant "OPENAI_API_KEY admitted for codex" now targets the reflowed tuple line.

**Red first:** `runs/145` (against `0ee767a3`, the new test bindings fail on the glued names and on `WEB_HOST` / `EDITOR_URL`) and `runs/146` (the relabel mutants ALIVE at `0ee767a3`: the MAJOR, reproduced).

## The Gemini key road (14 Oct, 20:21 EDT)

The road mirrors the Claude1 walk-token road, which passed review.

**`run_take.WALK_LOGIN_FILES`** gains `"r4-g": "/home/javier/gap4-secrets/r4-g/gemini-walk-login"`.
- `r4-g` is Javier's Google project label from the plan; the code named no other alias.
- The file holds one line, `GEMINI_API_KEY=<the study key>`.
- The name `gemini-walk-login` was chosen because it is not secret-shaped.

**The scripts** (`lane-reports/walk-login/`):
- `prepare_walk_login.py` and `verify_walk_login.py` now work from one `ACCOUNTS` table:
  - `claude1` → `claude-walk-token` holding `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN=`;
  - `r4-g` → `gemini-walk-login` holding `GEMINI_API_KEY=`.
- Account names may carry a hyphen, and an account not in the table is refused.
- `verify_walk_login.py` keeps every earlier yes/no check, using the account's own prefix, and adds two:
  - `quote in the value`: any `'` or `"` fails it;
  - `stray non-ASCII character`: the first such character is named by its Unicode name only, for example `ALMOST EQUAL TO` for U+2248 from Option-X. It is never shown.
- The `format ok` rule stays `read_login`'s: printable ASCII, no whitespace, no double quote.

**Tests** (`test_walk_login_scripts.GeminiKey`):
- prepare, paste and verify: `RESULT: OK`, and `run_take.read_login(path, "GEMINI_API_KEY")` agrees;
- each wrong shape fails, by name and without the planted value: a `≈`, a single or quoted value, a space, a doubled paste, the variable name pasted in, and Claude's prefix in the Gemini file;
- an unknown account is refused by both scripts.

**Red first** (`runs/150`, against `3a0d7f5a`): the scripts refuse `r4-g`, and `WALK_LOGIN_FILES` has no Gemini entry.

**Mutants** (`walk-login/verify_mutants.py`, now over both test classes; `runs/151`): 8 of 8 red, none alive.
- New ones: quotes unchecked, stray non-ASCII unchecked, the stray named by the character instead of its name, and the Gemini account unknown.
- One named equivalent: the stray line's own result gate, because a non-ASCII byte already fails `format ok`.

**Deviation, for glitch-06's ruling: the "gate closed as for Codex" is not committed.**
- I built it: a closed `GEMINI_KEY_READY` that refuses a Gemini exec, after the refused-name checks and before any read of the key. It had its test and four red mutants.
- With it, the harness lane's `test_gemini_adapter` went from 138 OK to 55 failures, every one "NotReady: Gemini key binding not ready" (`runs/153-gemini-adapter-with-the-exec-gate-set-aside.txt`). That module is the harness lane's, and making it pass needs its tests to hold the gate open.
- So I set the gate aside, with its diff kept, rather than break another lane's suite. It is ready to apply once the harness lane is coordinated.
- Without it, a Gemini exec is still governed by every other check: the row, the model pin, the login file's place and form, and the refused names.

**Runner counts** (`runs/154-*`, Mac, light gate passed before each module, one at a time):
- OK: test_run_take 57, test_env_allowlist 28, test_preflight 59, test_stub_take 19, test_stub_agents 18, test_live_box 38, test_claude_code_adapter 19, test_gemini_adapter 138, test_wave4_bindings 13, test_take_config 9, test_drive_commit 31, test_box_integration 61, test_walk_login_scripts 5.
- Baselines, unchanged: test_codex_adapter 13 failures (the same 13 as `runs/122`), test_walk_builders 3, test_walk_runner 46.

**A slip, mine:** while the gate was still in, I edited `run_take.py` during a sweep. I stopped that sweep by pid, deleted its run files, kept only the `test_gemini_adapter` log as the gate's evidence, and reran everything once in sequence (`runs/154`).

**The card:** `plans/gap-study-round-4/gemini-key-handoff.md`, in the Glitch brain; it is not in this clone.
- Its sources are Gemini CLI's own docs at the pinned tag `v0.64.0`:
  - `docs/get-started/authentication.mdx`, sha256 `8a2eb391…`;
  - `docs/resources/quota-and-pricing.md`, sha256 `b843e3c2…`.
- What it says, and nothing more:
  - the key comes from Google AI Studio and is set as `GEMINI_API_KEY`;
  - in headless mode, no Google Cloud project is needed for the CLI itself;
  - an API key pays per token or call;
  - a Cloud console key belongs to Vertex AI with `GOOGLE_API_KEY`, which the box refuses.
- That AI Studio offers a project choice for the key is said as a condition ("if AI Studio asks"). These docs do not show it.

## Why the Gemini exec gate stays out (glitch-06's ruling, 14 Oct, 20:45 EDT)

glitch-06 confirmed that setting the gate aside was right. It is not committed, for two reasons:
- **The gate for a Gemini walk is on the harness side.** `walk_builders.prepare` admits only Claude Code today, so a Gemini walk fails closed there.
- **Before Gemini is admitted,** the harness lane's Gemini wiring must bind the exact-value mask to `WALK_LOGIN_FILES["r4-g"]`, with a test that a planted key is masked, or refused at commit.

**The saved diff,** kept here as the record.
- It is the working tree against `3a0d7f5a` when the gate stood after the refused-name checks.
- Its `WALK_LOGIN_FILES` hunk is already in `1d32399b`.
- The rest is the gate, its two `filled()` openings, and `TestGeminiKeyGate`.

```diff
diff --git a/evals/gap-study-4/box/run_take.py b/evals/gap-study-4/box/run_take.py

 # long-lived token" and "Authentication precedence" item 5; env-vars.md). Prepared and verified by
 # lane-reports/walk-login/prepare_walk_login.py and verify_walk_login.py; the value is never read by any session.
-WALK_LOGIN_FILES = {"claude1": "/home/javier/gap4-secrets/claude1/claude-walk-token"}
+# The Gemini key road (glitch-06, 14 Oct 2026), the same road: account alias r4-g (Javier's Google project label from the
+# plan), one line `GEMINI_API_KEY=<the study key>`, the variable the Gemini arm's row names; prepared and verified by
+# the same two scripts. A Gemini exec stays behind GEMINI_KEY_READY (below) until the key is pasted and a walk passes.
+WALK_LOGIN_FILES = {"claude1": "/home/javier/gap4-secrets/claude1/claude-walk-token",
+                    "r4-g": "/home/javier/gap4-secrets/r4-g/gemini-walk-login"}
+# The Gemini key road's gate (glitch-06, 14 Oct 2026): CLOSED, as the Codex lease's is; exec_argv refuses a Gemini exec
+# with NotReady(GEMINI_KEY_NOT_READY) before any login is read, until the study key is pasted into
+# WALK_LOGIN_FILES["r4-g"] and the first Gemini walk passes.
+GEMINI_KEY_NOT_READY = "Gemini key binding not ready: the study key waits for its paste and the first walk"
+GEMINI_KEY_READY = False
 
 # The bypass acceptance: RULED not required in -p (glitch-06, 14 Oct 2026, on the docs read in Addendum E of
 # gap4-codex-rollout-audit-20261014/codex-0.162.1-rollout-audit.md). The study sends NO bypass acceptance key.
@@ -943,6 +952,8 @@ def exec_argv(container_id: str, inner, *, user: str, workdir: str, harness: str
     login = LOGIN_VARIABLE[harness]
     # Refused names first, from the table alone (so a cross-arm refusal never waits on a PENDING value).
     check_environment(harness, src, set(TAKE_ENV[harness]))
+    if harness == GEMINI and GEMINI_KEY_READY is not True:     # the Gemini key gate: after the refusals, before any read
+        raise NotReady(GEMINI_KEY_NOT_READY)
     if user != AGENT_USER:
         raise Refused(f"every exec into the take container runs as the agent, {AGENT_USER}")
     _check_id(container_id, "container id")

+class TestGeminiKeyGate(MutantCase):
+    """glitch-06 (14 Oct 2026): the Gemini key road's gate, CLOSED in the committed module, as the Codex lease's is: a
+    Gemini exec refuses NotReady(GEMINI_KEY_NOT_READY) before the login is read, until the study key is pasted and the
+    first Gemini walk passes."""
+
+    def check(self):
+        out = []
+        if rt.GEMINI_KEY_READY is not False:
+            out.append("the Gemini key gate is not closed in the committed module")
+        d = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="w-")
+        os.chmod(d, 0o700)
+        lf = os.path.join(d, "gemini-walk-login")
+        fd = os.open(lf, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
+        os.write(fd, b"GEMINI_API_KEY=" + FAKE_VALUE.encode() + b"\n")
+        os.close(fd)
+        inner = ["gemini", "-m", "gemini-3.8-flash"]
+        with mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=True), \
+                mock.patch.object(rt, "read_login", side_effect=AssertionError("login read")):
+            try:
+                rt.exec_argv(ID["take"], inner, user=rt.AGENT_USER, workdir="/", harness=rt.GEMINI,
+                             run_tree="/srv/w/t1/tree", login_file=lf, caller_env={"PATH": "/bin"})
+                out.append("a Gemini exec was built with the key gate closed")
+            except rt.NotReady as e:
+                if str(e) != rt.GEMINI_KEY_NOT_READY:
+                    out.append(f"the closed gate refused with another message: {e}")
+            except AssertionError:
+                out.append("the login was read before the closed gate refused")
+        with mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=True), filled():
+            try:
+                argv, env = rt.exec_argv(ID["take"], inner, user=rt.AGENT_USER, workdir="/", harness=rt.GEMINI,
+                                         run_tree="/srv/w/t1/tree", login_file=lf, caller_env={"PATH": "/bin"})
+                if env.get("GEMINI_API_KEY") != FAKE_VALUE or FAKE_VALUE in " ".join(argv):
+                    out.append("the open gate's exec does not carry the key by name only")
+            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
+                out.append(f"the open gate's exec failed: {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:120]}")
+        return out
+
+    def test_the_gemini_key_gate(self):
+        self.assertEqual(problems_of(self.check), [])
+
+
```

## run_take's Codex exec branch, behind the closed gate (14 Oct, 20:54 EDT)

**The change** (`box/run_take.py`): `exec_argv` for a Codex row now calls `_codex_exec_argv` in place of an unconditional NotReady.

**`_codex_exec_argv`** runs, in order:
1. **The closed lease gate,** first: NotReady(CODEX_SUBSCRIPTION_NOT_READY) before anything is checked or read. A bad lease, user or turn never shows ahead of it.
2. **The frame:** the agent user, the container id, the argv as a list of strings, and an absolute workdir.
3. **`check_codex_turn`.**
4. **The refused names** over the Codex row's values and over this process's and the caller's environments. That includes every sweep name and the broker-snapshot prefixes.
5. **`check_codex_lease_place`.**
6. **The argv:** `docker exec --user <agent> --workdir <wd> <id>` followed by the **no-login launcher** (`NO_LOGIN`) over exactly the Codex row's values. In those values, `CODEX_HOME` is `CONFIG_MOUNT`, the per-take config folder, which holds the lease `place_lease` put there.
7. **The client environment:** `runtime_env` alone. No credential name or value rides in argv or env.

**`check_codex_turn`** admits only what the pinned 0.162.1 help shows and the study uses.
- **Evidence:** `lane-reports/live-box-evidence-2026-10-13/codex-exec-help.json` (sha256 `a90c28e8972bacc34e6d46d560d071232d62b74f842b545a23d19cd81b5ac512`) and `codex-resume-help.json` (sha256 `f145e5b7415fa34eb74f0ec3af264f35d08bed9263b605170133520db1876450`). Both are `codex exec [resume] --help` run in the take-o image, and this commit adds both to the repo.
- **Usage:** `codex exec [OPTIONS] [PROMPT]` and `codex exec [OPTIONS] <COMMAND> [ARGS]`, where COMMAND is `resume`, whose usage is `codex exec resume [OPTIONS] [SESSION_ID] [PROMPT]`. Exec's options come before `resume`.
- **What it admits:**
  - the client `codex`, by name, as the pinned image's client;
  - the subcommand `exec`;
  - each of these exactly once, in any order:
    - `--json` ("Print events to stdout as JSONL");
    - `-m`/`--model <MODEL>` ("Model the agent should use");
    - `--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox` ("Skip all confirmation prompts and execute commands without sandboxing. ... Intended solely for running in environments that are externally sandboxed": the box is that sandbox, as in `codex_adapter.MODE_FLAGS["bypass"]`). **This departs from round 3:**
      - round 3 pinned `driver_constants.permission_mode` to `"default"` (`evals/gap-study-3/prereg.json`);
      - the pre-registered change is `prereg-draft.json` `limitations[0].round_4_reading`: "The condition is the box: each harness runs inside it in the permission mode its pin profile names" (`permission_mode: "pin-profile"`).
      - The Codex `permission_mode` pins are still PENDING (`prereg-draft.json` `version_pins.codex.<model>.permission_mode`).
      - A pin other than bypass makes `run_take` refuse the turn, because a turn without the bypass flag is refused. The adapter's `MODE_FLAGS` also knows only `bypass`.
  - then exactly one prompt, or `resume <session id> <prompt>`.
- **The model** must be one of `CODEX_MODELS` (`gpt-6.1-sol`, `gpt-6-luna`), bound by test to `prereg-draft.json` `models["O"]`.
- **The prompt** never starts with `-`: the help says a missing prompt, or `-`, reads stdin.
- **The session id** must match the adapter's thread-id form.
- **Refused:** every other option the help lists, a `--model=` glued value, `--last`, any option after `resume`, `fork`, `review`, `help`, and an absolute client path.

**`check_codex_lease_place`** checks the lease in place and never reads it. It refuses unless all of these hold:
- the path is absolute and named `auth.json` (`LEASE_FILE`);
- `check_login_place` passes: one link, outside the run tree, in the reading user's own mode-700 folder, never a link;
- the file is regular, mode 600, owned by the reading user, and outside every repository.

**Test** (`TestCodexExec`):

## The review of 1e10e320: CHANGES (one MAJOR), and the folds (14 Oct, 21:12 EDT)

**MAJOR: a one-word prompt that is a subcommand name.**
- `review`, `help` or `fork` as the only prompt passed `check_codex_turn` and built a full exec argv. The help's usage is `codex exec [OPTIONS] <COMMAND> [ARGS]`, so clap would run it as that subcommand.
- **The fix:** `CODEX_HELP_COMMANDS` holds the pinned help's Commands list, `("resume", "fork", "review", "help")`, and a non-resume prompt equal to any of them is refused, by name.
- **Tests:** one refusal per word as the only prompt, plus `resume` alone.

- **Residual, named:** hidden aliases, and subcommands clap infers that the help does not list (a prefix or a short alias, say), are not refused. Only the listed names are.

**For the harness lane, the same hole in `codex_adapter.argv`** (`codex_adapter.py:215`, harness-owned and not edited here; glitch-06 routes it). The exact change is to add, after its `line.startswith("-")` check:

```python

```

On a later turn, the line comes after `resume <thread id>`, which takes no subcommand, so the check is for the first turn only.

It also says the Codex `permission_mode` pins are PENDING, and that a pin other than bypass makes `run_take` refuse.

**MINOR 3 and NIT 5:** `TestCodexExec` now requires two equalities:
- `run_take._CODEX_SESSION.pattern == codex_adapter._THREAD_ID.pattern`;
- `(rt.CODEX_BYPASS_FLAG,) == codex_adapter.MODE_FLAGS["bypass"]`.

Each has a red mutant: the session form drifting, and the bypass flag drifting.

**NIT 4:** the comment by `LOGIN_VARIABLE` now reads "exec_argv hands Codex to _codex_exec_argv behind codex_lease_gate".

**A latent finding, for the harness lane** (fail-closed, not unsafe): the driver's evidence folder takes its mode from the process umask. ## Claude Code 2.1.296 static read: the environment vocabulary and the origin kinds (15 Oct, 01:21 EDT, clock read first)

**The ask** (glitch-6b, input to `plans/gap-study-round-4/first-walk-bootstrap-ruling-2026-10-15.md`): read the pinned binary with no model, no login and no network, by the static method of the Codex sweep (`06775d56`).
- (a) The environment names that drive.py's environment_record patterns match, diffed against round 3.
- (b) Whether the origin kind `task-notification` occurs, and what sits beside it.

**Method.**
- Four probes in `stub-take-20261015/probes/`, each fed to athena over ssh stdin as javier.
- Each copies `claude.exe` out of the pinned take image (`box/image.lock` image_id `sha256:336aa8cd…`, unchanged `1cde8066..383e5c2f`) with `docker create --network none` and `docker cp`, and never runs it. The container is removed, and the work folder is deleted and checked absent.
- Each was gated by `athena-gate.sh`: no athena-suite process and no javier container.
- Binary sha256 `24972e3bc859fab2b46ed4c1e51f7d6130f06d3bd550811a114640de3370d0de`, 257068216 bytes, the same in all four reads.
- `box/image.lock` does not record this sha256; it is bound here only through the image id.

**Baseline:** the 2.1.267 binary is not on athena, so this is not a binary-to-binary diff.
Every name is placed against round 3's **registered** vocabulary: `gap-study-3/prereg.json` environment_record, "read from the 2.1.267 binary", with its patterns and its API-key, billing-route and subscription-token lists.
All 14 listed names are still present in 2.1.296.

**(a) The finding that may change the ruling: new billing-route switches outside every pattern.**
- The binary's provider selector maps `anthropicAws`, `anthropicGoogleCloud` and `mantle` to `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_ANTHROPIC_AWS`, `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_ANTHROPIC_GOOGLE_CLOUD` and `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_MANTLE`, beside bedrock, vertex and foundry.
- `CLAUDE_CODE_USE_GATEWAY` routes a session through a gateway "with ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL and ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN".
- `CLAUDE_CODE_PROVIDER_MANAGED_BY_HOST` and `CLAUDE_CODE_HOST_CREDS_FILE` (a credentials-file path) gate a host-managed gateway.
- **None of the six matches drive.py's harness patterns** (`^CLAUDE_CODE_USE_(BEDROCK|VERTEX|FOUNDRY)$`) **or the generic pattern.** So environment_record would not list them, and the money line would not stop on them.

**Also on no stop list, though recorded by `^ANTHROPIC_` or the generic pattern:**
- Provider endpoints: `ANTHROPIC_{BEDROCK,VERTEX,FOUNDRY,AWS,GOOGLE_CLOUD,BEDROCK_MANTLE}_BASE_URL` and `ANTHROPIC_FOUNDRY_RESOURCE`.
- `ANTHROPIC_UNIX_SOCKET`, which the base-URL check reads beside `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`.
- Inference credentials:
  - `ANTHROPIC_FOUNDRY_AUTH_TOKEN`, named beside `ANTHROPIC_FOUNDRY_API_KEY` in its own refresh message;
  - `ANTHROPIC_IDENTITY_TOKEN` and `ANTHROPIC_IDENTITY_TOKEN_FILE` (OIDC federation);
  - `ANTHROPIC_ENVIRONMENT_KEY` and `ANTHROPIC_WORK_SECRET`;
  - `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FILE_DESCRIPTOR`, the subscription token by file descriptor, which is not on the subscription list;
  - `CLAUDE_CODE_GATEWAY_TOKEN`.
- Nine product-feature tokens (bridge, trusted device, messaging, memory, artifacts, HFI, Slack, MCP serve), none of them an inference route on this read.

The partition of all 387 names is in `stub-take-20261015/claude-2.1.296-env-classification-20261015.json`: 161 harness, 174 generic, 52 outside (from the route read).

**(b) The origin kinds.**
- **`task-notification` is present:** 15 `origin:{kind:"task-notification"}` writers, 35 `{kind:"task-notification"` objects and 21 readers.
  It carries a `source` (for example `goal-checkin` and `worker-checkin`).
  A record with no `origin` whose `commandMode` is `"task-notification"` is given that origin (`YIt`).
- **The binary's own classifier** (`Q$e`) sorts a user record's origin into frames:
  - `isMeta` without provenance is invisible.
  - compact summary is summary.
  - `peer` from a slack bot, and projects-relay, are invisible.
  - relay-human.
  - **no origin is `human`/`unstamped`.**
  - **`human` and `auto-continuation` are both framed `human`.**
  - `task-notification`, `coordinator`, `channel`, `peer`, `slack-ping`, `observer`, `observer-activity`, `plugin` and `unclassified` are framed `non-user`, and any other kind becomes `unclassified`.
- **Other writer kinds:** `auto-continuation` (10), `channel` (3), `composer` (1), `human` (12), `observer-activity` (1), `peer` (1), `person` (3), `plugin` (8), `remote` (1), `suggestion` (1), `unclassified` (1).
- **The point for the ruling:** `auto-continuation` (the plan-mode "Implement this plan." continuation) is a user record the harness writes itself, and the binary frames it as **human**. If the checker exempts only `CC_TASK_NOTIFICATION` as harness-delivered, an `auto-continuation` record would read as an operator line.

**Records:**
- `claude-2.1.296-binary-sweep-20261015.json`
- `claude-2.1.296-origin-kinds-20261015.json`
- `claude-2.1.296-route-names-20261015.json`

## Item 2: machine_gate redesigned athena-side (15 Oct, 01:40 EDT, glitch-6b's ruling, clock read first)

**Base.** glitch-6b ruled machine_gate (harness `9425bf62`) and the launch road to this lane, and both live on the merged tree.
So the box branch first merged the integration head `383e5c2f` (`988f24ee`: a clean merge, whose tree differs from `383e5c2f` only by this lane's report commits).

**The ruling:**
- The walk runs ON athena, so the gate that admits a take is athena-side.
- It admits only when no process runs under the athena-suite account AND `/srv/aegis-handoff/machine/reserved.json` is absent or held by this walk's own hold, matched exactly by its id. Anything unreadable refuses.
- On the Mac side:
  - `launched=busy` never ran.
  - A `started` run without a verdict is read by `athena_suite.py status`, and a status that cannot be read fails closed.
  - The walk's own hold never blocks itself.

**`machine_gate.py`, rewritten:**
- **The athena gate:**
  - `suite_account_pids` scans /proc for the account's uid; an unknown account or an unreadable /proc gives None.
  - `read_reservation` gives absent, held(by) or unreadable. Unreadable covers a link, a folder, more than 4 KiB, a non-object, and a `by` that is not athena-reserve's BY_RE.
  - `athena_problems` and `athena_gate` combine the two.
  - `gate_from_env` reads `GAP4_SUITE_ACCOUNT`, `GAP4_RESERVATION` and `GAP4_OWN_HOLD`, which the launch road sets on athena under `athena-reserve hold --by <GAP4_OWN_HOLD>`.
  - It never reads the suite list.
- **The Mac pre-check:** `list_problems(list_text, status_of)`, `suite_status_reader` and `mac_precheck`.
  - ENDED_STATES are done, failed, busy, lost and missing. `running`, `prepared` and any unknown word are in progress.
- **CLI:** `mac-precheck <athena_suite.py>` and `athena-check`.
- **The Mac's `.machine-reserved` booking is dropped,** because the walk never runs on the Mac.
- **Another hold refuses even when it has expired.** That is the ruling read literally, a strict departure from athena_suite's reading, where a reservation past its `until` is free.

**Tests** (`tests/test_machine_gate.py`, rewritten):
- clauses A1-A4 (athena) and M1-M3 (Mac);
- 8 witnesses: busy-runs, foreign-hold-admitted, unreadable-reservation-admitted, unread-processes-admitted, status-error-swallowed, link-followed, dead-proc-is-quiet, old-env-builds.
- **M1** replays the real 15 Oct list. It is `tests/fixtures/machine-gate-athena-suite-list-20261015.txt`: 1202 lines, names and commits masked, claimed in this lane's COPIED fragment.
  - The 844 busy runs are never queried.
  - Exactly the 9 started runs without a verdict are queried.
  - One `running` status is the one problem.
- **A4** binds the athena fail-open: the first gate's variables, with a list that prints nothing, never build a gate.

## Item 3: the walk launch road, `box/walk_launch.py` (15 Oct, 01:48 EDT, clock read first)

**The ruling:** a git bundle out and back.
- On the Mac, the bundle carries the whole history of the merged head (the pinned tree `844a4ce0` included). It is gitleaks-scanned, then copied by scp.
- athena clones the bundle and runs the walk.
- Coming back, a bundle of the new commits only is copied to the Mac, gitleaks-scanned, then fetched into a Mac-side walk-records clone.
- The script sets umask 0022 and installs or streams `athena-reserve` the way the runbook does.
- Nothing is pushed, and no new login is placed.

**Verbs:**
- **ship:**
  1. the Mac pre-check (`machine_gate.mac_precheck`);
  2. `PINNED_TREE` must be an ancestor of the head;
  3. `git bundle create <ref>` and `verify`;
  4. a clone of the bundle, whose head must be the ref's;
  5. gitleaks over that clone. Any finding outside `KNOWN_HISTORY_FINDINGS` refuses, and nothing has touched athena before this point.
  6. On athena: `mktemp -d /home/javier/w-XXXXXXXX` (umask 077); the bundle and athena-reserve are copied and each sha256 read back; then the athena clone, whose head must be the head.
- **run:** one remote command.
  - It opens with `umask 0022; set -e; test ! -e <reserved.json>`, because a hold would overwrite another reservation.
  - Then `machine_gate athena-check`, with the gate's three variables.
  - Then `nohup sh -c 'python3 <child>/athena-reserve hold --by gap4-walk-<id> -- env <the three variables> python3 -B <child>/repo/evals/gap-study-4/walk_runner.py …; echo $? > walk.exit'`, with the pid recorded.
- **status:** `running`, `exit=<code>` or `lost`.
- **fetch:**
  1. It refuses while the walk is running, and reports "no commit" when there is nothing new.
  2. The tip must descend from the head.
  3. On athena: `bundle create <head>..<ref>`, and its sha256 is read back after the copy.
  4. On the Mac: a scratch shared clone of the records clone fetches the bundle, and gitleaks runs over exactly `<head>..<tip>` with **no allowance**.
  5. Only then is it fetched into the records clone as `walk/<child>`.

**How athena-reserve gets there:** it is copied into the child and checked against the runbook's pinned sha256 (`efeecb3a…`, which the Mac copy matches).
It is not streamed over stdin, because a detached (`&`) job's stdin is /dev/null, so the script could not be read once the job is in the background. The copy is checked by sha256 like the bundle.

**KNOWN_HISTORY_FINDINGS** (the outward bundle's only allowance), measured at 01:40 on a full-history bundle of `fa0838ec`:
- 2586 commits, 15 report entries, 8 distinct fingerprints, all `generic-api-key`.
- Seven are in round 1 and round 2 study transcripts. Those are records: a finding on one is ruled, never edited out.
- One is in `docs/environment.md` line 156, the owner-ruled prose false positive.
- Every one of the 8 commits is on `origin/` refs of the GARS clone (`workspaces/genomics-agentic-research-system`, origin github.com/javrodriguez/genomics-agentic-research-system), as last fetched.
  So they are already public, and copying them to athena discloses nothing new.
- **This is my reading, not an owner ruling.** It is flagged for glitch-6b to confirm or overrule.
- A test binds each fingerprint's commit to this repository's history.

**Tests** (`tests/test_walk_launch.py`, fakes: real local git on temporary repositories, a fake athena keyed by command shape, a fake gitleaks):
- clauses L1 ship, L2 run, L3 fetch (a real `<head>..main` bundle fetched into a real records clone) and L4 bounds;
- 7 witnesses: scan-skipped, unknown-finding-admitted, no-umask, hold-dropped, reservation-not-checked, back-range-whole-history, back-scan-allowance.

**Red first:** HEAD `fa0838ec` with only the new test gave `ImportError: cannot import name 'walk_launch'` (01:45).
**Green** (Mac, light-ok exit 0): Ran 7, OK, every witness red (01:47).
**copy_manifest --check:** ok, 417 files.

**Not exercised:** a real ship, run or fetch against athena. athena is running interim2, and the first real use is the walk itself.
- **I recommend** a dry `ship` by glitch-6b's word once athena is idle. It moves only the bundle and athena-reserve into a new child.

**The ruling:** content rulings are Javier's, so glitch-6b did not rule the 8 findings safe. Instead:

## Review of 58013b43..ad71ca07: CHANGES, folded (15 Oct, from 02:33 EDT, clock read first)

### MAJOR 1: the return scan is athena_suite's scan_unpublished with no allowance

**Reproduced first** with the real gitleaks against HEAD `ad71ca07`'s `fetch` (scratch `repro_m1.py`, 02:32). Each of these was **FETCHED**, and the walk branch was created:
- a `# gitleaks:allow` line;
- a token in a commit message;
- a token inside a `.json.gz` blob.

**The fix.** `fetch` takes `suite` / `suite_module` and runs `scan_unpublished(scratch, tip, work, gitleaks, [head], None, (), None, None, None)` on the scratch clone at the tip.
- That brings `--ignore-gitleaks-allow`, an empty ignore file, remerge diffs, the commit-message scan and the binary-blob refusal, with no allowance.
- Any exception, or 0 commits scanned, refuses.
- `scan_findings` (the plain `gitleaks git --log-opts` scan) is removed.

**Tests:**
- **L3 (fake athena_suite):** the scan's arguments are bound (tip, `[head]`, no rulings, blobs or messages). No plain gitleaks call is made, and the scan comes before the records fetch.
- **New `TestRealReturnScan`,** with the real athena_suite.py and the real gitleaks, synthetic tokens made at run time:
  - the allow line refuses (`n.txt:github-pat:1`);
  - the message refuses (`commit message …`);
  - the `.json.gz` refuses (`1 binary blob(s) the scanner cannot read`);
  - none leaves a walk branch, and a clean commit is fetched.
  - **Named:** where athena_suite.py or gitleaks is absent (the athena battery's clone holds no aegis checkout), this class is skipped by name and never passed.
- **Witnesses:** back-scan-allowance (the push door's rulings passed back in) and back-scan-plain.

**Red:** Ran 11, errors=9 (`fetch() got an unexpected keyword argument 'suite'`), plus the behavioural reproduction above.
**Green:** Mac, 02:32: Ran 11, OK.

### MINOR 2, MINOR 3 and NIT 6: the athena gate sees runs that hold no suite-account process, and proves it can see (02:37)

**MINOR 2.** `athena_suite_remote.work` runs as javier and reaches the athena-suite account only through sudo calls, so a live run can have zero account processes. The gate now also refuses on two more signals:
- **a held slot lock:** `held_slot_locks`, a non-blocking `flock` probe on each `~/athena-suites/.slots/<n>.lock`, released at once. An unreadable folder or lock gives None, which refuses.
- **a live runner on any account:** `remote_runner_pids`, which reads every visible `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` for an `athena_suite_remote.py` … `work` argv. An unreadable argv gives None, which refuses; an ended process is skipped.
- `GAP4_SUITE_SLOTS` joins the environment, and `walk_launch.run_command` passes `/home/javier/athena-suites/.slots`.

**MINOR 3.** `processes_visible` requires a stat of `/proc/1`, root's init. Under `hidepid=invisible`, a reader without aegis-procview cannot stat it, so the gate refuses rather than trust an empty scan.

**NIT 6.** A test binds that a `PermissionError` on `/proc/<pid>` gives None, not a partial list.

**Tests:**
- A1: invisible table, held slot, unreadable slots, live runner, unreadable argv.
- A3, on a fake /proc and real lock files:
  - a slot `flock`'d by the test itself is seen and closes the gate;
  - an `athena_suite_remote.py work` argv is seen;
  - a hidden /proc/1 closes the gate;
  - a `cmdline` that cannot be read refuses.
- New witnesses: invisible-table-admitted, slot-locks-ignored, runner-ignored, runner-argv-unread-quiet, visibility-unchecked, pid-stat-error-quiet.

### MINOR 5 and NIT 7: every launch-road guard has a red case; the reservation is claimed with O_EXCL (02:55)

**MINOR 5, new clause L5-guards.** Each guard refuses with its own message:
- the local athena-reserve pin;
- `git bundle list-heads` (a fake list-heads naming another head);
- the outward sha256 read-back (athena misreports walk-out.bundle);
- fetch's `merge-base --is-ancestor head tip` (exit 1);
- the back bundle's sha256 (athena misreports walk-back.bundle);
- `got != tip` (athena names another tip than the bundle's).

A fetch refusal also must leave no walk branch. Six witnesses, one per guard, are each red: reserve-pin-unchecked, list-heads-unchecked, outward-sha-unchecked, back-ancestry-unchecked, back-sha-unchecked, back-tip-unchecked.

**NIT 7.**
- **The claim:** `run` no longer runs `test ! -e reserved.json`. It claims the reservation itself: `python3 -I -c` creates it with `O_EXCL`, carrying `{"by": <hold id>, "until": now + 29 min}`, so one already there refuses the start.
- **The gate:** a closed gate removes the claim (`|| { rm -f reserved.json; exit 1; }`). `hold` then rewrites the reservation with the same id and removes it when the walk ends.
- **Documented in the module docstring ("THE HOLD'S LIMITS"):**
  - **the race:** `athena-reserve set` and `hold` write unconditionally, so a later writer overwrites the claim unseen;
  - **SIGHUP:** `nohup sh -c` covers both the hold and the walk;
  - **lease lapse, a named residual:** if the hold is SIGKILLed, the 29-minute lease is not renewed. The reservation then reads free while the walk keeps running, and athena_suite could admit a suite beside it.

**L2** now runs the claim's own program on a temporary path: the first claim creates the file with the hold id, and the second refuses. New witnesses: claim-dropped, claim-not-exclusive, claim-left-on-closed-gate.

**Red:** the new tests against HEAD `7d515916`'s walk_launch fail L2 (the O_EXCL claim) and the three claim witnesses.

## glitch-6b's ruling (b): the watcher's gzip evidence blobs ship decoded and scanned (15 Oct, 09:50 EDT, clock read first)

**The ruling:**
- A binary blob in head..tip ships ONLY if it is a gzip stream under `evidence/blobs/` that decompresses cleanly within a size bound.
- The decompressed bytes are scanned in a private temp folder by the same gitleaks, with no allowance, `--ignore-gitleaks-allow` and an empty ignore file.
- Exactly those blob ids, and only if clean, go to `scan_unpublished` as `allow_blobs`. Everything else refuses.
- No decompressed content is ever printed.

**The change** (`box/walk_launch.py`):
- **`gunzip_bounded`:** one gzip member, at most 16 MiB decompressed (`BLOB_MAX_BYTES`), at most 256:1 (`BLOB_MAX_RATIO`). It refuses a decode error, overrun, truncation, trailing bytes, or a decompressed payload that is itself binary (a NUL in its first 8000 bytes, which the text scan could not read).
- **`evidence_blobs`:** every binary blob from athena_suite's own `binary_blobs_hashed(scratch, tip, [head])` must be:
  - absent from the shipped head's whole history (`rev-list --objects head`);
  - at EVERY path it appears in, in the walk's commits (`ls-tree -r` of each commit in head..tip), at `evals/gap-study-4/rehearsals/<walk>/evidence/blobs/<sha256>`;
  - decoded by `gunzip_bounded`.
- **`scan_decompressed`:** `gitleaks dir --redact --exit-code 0 --ignore-gitleaks-allow --gitleaks-ignore-path <empty>` over the decompressed files, written mode 600 under a 0700 `mkdtemp`. The folder is removed however the scan ends. It returns only the blob ids with a finding.
- **`admitted_evidence_blobs`** feeds `fetch`'s `scan_unpublished(..., allow_blobs=<admitted ids>, ...)`. The rulings, ruled binaries and ruled messages stay None.

**Tests:**
- **New L8-blobs** runs everywhere (real git, a fake athena_suite reader with the NUL rule, a fake gitleaks that flags `PLANTED`):
  - the decoder refuses: non-gzip, a 20 MiB bomb, a 300 KiB ratio bomb, trailing bytes, truncation, a binary payload;
  - `admitted_evidence_blobs` admits a clean evidence gzip, and refuses a planted one, a non-gzip, a bomb, a gzip outside `evidence/blobs/`, the same blob also at a second path outside, and a blob already in the head's history;
  - no refusal prints content, and no decompressed folder is left.
- **Witnesses, each red:** blob-bound-dropped, blob-path-unchecked, blob-head-history-unchecked, decompressed-findings-ignored, trailing-bytes-admitted.
- **Named equivalent (no witness):** the 1f 8b magic check. zlib's gzip mode refuses a stream without the header itself, so L8 stays green with the check dropped.
- **`TestRealReturnScan`** (Mac; real athena_suite, real gitleaks) has four new cases:
  - an evidence gzip holding a synthetic token refuses ("decompressed evidence blob");
  - a clean evidence gzip is fetched;
  - a non-gzip evidence blob refuses;
  - an evidence gzip bomb refuses.
  - The earlier `.json.gz` at the folder root stays as the outside-evidence case and still refuses.

**Red** (the new tests against HEAD `c31524ab`'s walk_launch, 09:50): Ran 21, failures=2, errors=6.
- **The old code refuses a clean evidence gzip that the new code passes,** because it refused every binary.
- On the token case it refused too, but for the binary, not the decompressed content.
- There is no case where the old code passes what the new code refuses: the old one refused all binaries.

### The review of b1f5c952 (CHANGES), folded (10:37)

**MAJOR 1: the gzip header fields.**
- `gzip_header` parses RFC 1952 itself:
  - the magic and the deflate method;
  - **any reserved FLG bit (0xE0) refuses**;
  - FEXTRA (XLEN plus its bytes), FNAME and FCOMMENT (NUL-terminated) are returned as fields;
  - **an FHCRC that does not validate** (crc32 of the header & 0xFFFF) **refuses**.
- `gunzip_to` inflates the raw deflate itself and checks the trailer's CRC32 and ISIZE.
- The fields go into a sibling 0600 file `<oid>.header`, scanned with the payload. A field holding a NUL refuses.

**MAJOR 2: memory.**
- New caps: `BLOB_TOTAL_MAX` (64 MiB decompressed per walk) and `BLOB_MAX_COUNT` (256 blobs).
- Each payload streams in 1 MiB pieces straight into its 0600 file; no dict of decompressed bytes exists.
- The stored (compressed) size is capped at 16 MiB too, checked with `cat-file -s` before reading.

**MINOR 3:** an absolute-cap bomb: about 17.3 MiB of base64 text, no NUL, gzip level 1 (about 1.3:1). Its own witness: absolute-cap-dropped.

**MINOR 4:** a real-scanner case, an allow line inside an evidence gzip, which refuses. The fake gitleaks in L8 now honours `gitleaks:allow` unless `--ignore-gitleaks-allow` is passed, so the witness allow-comments-honoured is red.

**MINOR 5:** `evidence_candidates` routes EVERY blob in head..tip that starts with 1f 8b, or that git would read as binary, through the evidence path.
- A real-scanner case: a 1f-8b blob git reads as text (`notes.txt`) refuses, never skipped.
- Witness: text-gzip-skipped.

**NIT 7: blob names.** The sidecar names a blob by the sha256 of the bytes it stores (`box/sidecar.py` snapshot_tree, `blobs[got[0]] = got[1]`, `got[0]` the sha256 of the file read). It stores the watched file's bytes as they are; the w001 blobs are gzip because the files the agent read were.
- Every path must therefore be `…/evidence/blobs/<sha256 of the blob's stored bytes>`. Witness: blob-name-unbound.

**Real-scanner cases** (Mac): a token in FEXTRA, FNAME or FCOMMENT refuses ("decompressed evidence blob"); the allow-line gzip refuses; the text-looking gzip refuses.

**L8 witnesses, each red:** blob-bound-dropped, absolute-cap-dropped, count-cap-dropped, blob-path-unchecked, blob-name-unbound, blob-head-history-unchecked, decompressed-findings-ignored, header-fields-unscanned, reserved-bits-admitted, fhcrc-unchecked, trailer-unchecked, text-gzip-skipped, allow-comments-honoured.
- Named equivalent: the 1f 8b magic check in gzip_header. The method byte and the raw inflate refuse a non-gzip without it.

## The drive-to-box operator seam (w013's launch refusal), 15 Oct 13:59 EDT, clock read first

**The refusal.** CP6 walk w013 (Haiku, confounded-design trap w1) refused at launch on `ce818d85`:
- the message: "the ownership road's operator_read is not operator_read(runtime, *, image, root_kind, root, rel_path, caller_env)";
- the source of that message: `drive.ownership_problems`.

**Which side holds the reviewed design: the box.** The fix belongs in drive.py, which is harness code, so this lane did not edit it.
- **The box's history:**
  - `box/ownership.py` added operator_read in `4c3d8e53` (14 Oct 09:45) and the config root in `d73c43ed` (12:14).
  - **`161d038d` (13:15) folded the reviewed O-3** of review-operator-read (d73c43ed PASS): "the root is chosen from the take's pair, both existing non-link folders, neither inside the other, the config folder outside every repository".
  - This report recorded the change at the time: "Both functions now take the take's pair (`run_tree`, `config_dir`) and choose the root by kind; the `root=` keyword is gone".
  - The box's own `tests/test_ownership.py` binds the old `root=` form as refused ("the old API").
- **The drive side:**
  - drive.py's contract and `OWNERSHIP_OPERATOR_READ_PARAMS` / `OWNERSHIP_OPERATOR_LIST_PARAMS` came in with harness `e3f5010b` (14 Oct 10:23) and `573b5a61` (14:50), with `root`.
  - `tests/test_box_integration.py`'s `FakeOwnership` (`operator_read(..., root_kind, root, rel_path, ...)`, `operator_list(...)`) carries the same stale signature.
  - That is why every battery stayed green: the fake agrees with drive, not with the box.

**Which tasks reach operator_read** (drive.py at `ce818d85`):
- **Mid-take operator steps:** `OPERATOR_STEPS` is `("explain-not-act", 2): operator-save` and `("confounded-design", 3): wait-for-samples-csv-then-copy-design`. Launch requires operator_write and operator_read for a row of those two tasks, so **every arm-A walk of explain-not-act and confounded-design refuses at launch**.
- **Every vendor take (arms O and G, all tasks):** these read the native session through `config_view` (operator_list + operator_read, root_kind "config"), and so refuse at launch too (`native=True`).
- **Arm-A walks of the other seven tasks are unaffected.** w001 (approval-scope) ran.

**The seam test** (`lane-reports/box-seams-20261015/test_drive_box_seams.py`):
- It is proposed for the harness lane's `tests/`. It sits outside the battery here, because it is red until drive.py changes.
- It drives drive's own code into the REAL box functions, with the box's own fake runtime (`test_ownership.Fake`):
  - **S1:** `TakeDriver._operator_read` gives the box's None for an absent file.
  - **S2:** `TakeDriver.config_view`'s reader and lister.
  - **S3:** `drive.ownership_problems(real box, operator_write=True, native=True)` and `drive.settings_guard_problems(real box)` are both empty.
  - **S4:** every drive.py call of a box function by name (`_ownership`, `_settings_guard`, `_preflight`, `_sidecar`, `_run_take`, `self.ownership`, `self.settings_guard`) binds to the real signature (ast plus `inspect.signature().bind`).

**Red at `ce818d85`** (`red-at-ce818d85.txt`): Ran 4, failures=2, errors=2.
- S1: `operator_read() got an unexpected keyword argument 'root'`, which surfaces as OperatorStepRefused.
- S2: SnapshotRefused, from the same cause.
- S3: operator_read and operator_list are both off.
- S4: `drive.py:2395 operator_read: missing a required argument: 'run_tree'`.

**Green with the proposed drive.py patch** (`drive-operator-seam-proposed.patch`, applied only in a scratch clone of `ce818d85`; `green-with-proposed-patch.txt`): Ran 4, OK.
- The patch changes the contract comment.
- It changes both PARAMS tuples to `(runtime, image, root_kind, run_tree, config_dir, rel_*, caller_env)`.
- `_operator_read` and `config_view` now pass `run_tree=…, config_dir=take.config_dir` in place of `root=`.

**The witness:** reverting the signature is the `ce818d85` code itself, and the test is red there. Once the harness lane carries the test, its own witness is the same revert.

**The other seams (S4 over the whole of drive.py at `ce818d85`):**
- 22 named drive-to-box calls bind.
- The one that does not is drive.py:2395.
- `config_view`'s `getattr(road, what)(...)` call is dynamic, so S4 cannot see it; S2 covers it.
- The settings guard's `baseline(run_tree, model)` and `snapshot_problems(snapshot, base)` match the box.
- grant, restore and operator_write match the box.

**For glitch-6b to route to the harness lane:**
1. Apply the drive.py patch.
2. Update `tests/test_box_integration.py`'s `FakeOwnership.operator_read` and `operator_list` to the box's signature.
3. Add `test_drive_box_seams.py` to `tests/`.

### The closure check on 76707728 (OPEN on MAJOR H and I), and the sibling review's two layers, folded (15 Oct, from 14:01 EDT, clock read first)

**MAJOR H: tree entries a diff omits.**
- Each new commit's paths unit is now every entry of `git ls-tree -r -t -z --full-tree <c>` (empty subtrees included) that no parent's listing holds; for a root commit, every entry. This is athena_suite's fix for the same hole, and it covers a merge (MAJOR G) too.
- A commit with 3 or more parents refuses by construction.
- **NIT, recorded:** `-z` only frames the listing and plays no security role.
- **NIT, recorded:** the octopus CONTENT refusal on the patch scan comes from athena_suite's `byte_ruling_for_finding` (athena_suite.py about lines 904-911 at aegis `5192466`), which this lane does not pin. The 3-parent refusal here no longer depends on it.

**MAJOR I: what a bundle may carry.**
- (b) `git bundle list-heads` of the back bundle must print exactly `<tip> refs/heads/<ref>`.
- (a) The scratch fetch uses `--no-tags`.
- (d) Every object the scratch holds beyond the records clone must be exactly the objects reachable from the tip and not from the head (`rev-list --objects`). An extra blob or tag object in the pack refuses before the scan.
- (c) The records clone is filled ONLY from the scanned scratch: `git -C records fetch -q --no-tags <scratch> refs/walk/back:refs/heads/walk/<name>`, never from the bundle. Afterwards, the objects records gained must equal that same reachable set.

**The sibling review of athena_suite: two layers.**
- (1) `git fsck --strict --no-dangling --no-progress` runs over the scratch before anything is scanned. Any output line, warnings included, or a non-zero exit refuses.
- (2) Every blob or tree record in every `ls-tree -r -t` listing has its real type read back with `cat-file --batch-check`; a mismatch refuses. A blob-mode entry naming a tree object (`hash-object --literally`) is listed as a blob by ls-tree.

**Tests:**
- **L8:**
  - an empty subtree named with a planted marker;
  - a clean octopus (refused for its parents only);
  - a disguised tree (`100644 innocent <tree id>`, inner folder planted), once with both layers and once with fsck patched aside;
  - a zero-padded mode (`040000 zdir`), which only fsck --strict reports;
  - the disguised tree's listing read directly by `_tree_entries`, which refuses.
- **L3:** a back bundle with an extra unreachable blob (packed by pack-objects) refuses, and so does one with an annotated tag ref (`refs/tags/evil`). Neither leaves a ref in records. On a clean fetch, the records fetch must name the scratch's `refs/walk/back` with `--no-tags`, never a bundle file.
- **L5:** the back bundle's heads.
- **Real gitleaks 8.30.0:** a token-named empty subtree refuses, and so does a token-named folder inside a disguised tree.
- **New witnesses, each red:**
  - empty-subtrees-dropped (no `-t`);
  - octopus-admitted;
  - extra-objects-admitted;
  - records-from-the-bundle;
  - back-heads-unchecked;
  - fsck-dropped;
  - entry-types-unchecked.
- **Named equivalent:** fetch's `got != tip` sits behind list-heads, which already requires the single head to be the tip. `back-tip-unchecked` is retired.
- `merge-paths-dropped` is retired with the diff-tree code. The merge plant itself still refuses through the parent subtraction.

**Red** (the new tests against `0ce4e442`'s walk_launch, which equals `76707728`'s; 14:04 and 15:12):
- The token-named empty subtree was **FETCHED**.
- An extra unreachable blob and an annotated tag ref were each **FETCHED**, and the tag ref landed in records.
- The records clone was fetched from the bundle.
- L8: the empty planted subtree, the clean octopus and the zero-padded mode all passed.
- **The disguised tree was NOT fetched on the old code.** It refused, by accident: `git cat-file failed (exit 128)`, the candidate scan's `cat-file blob` on a tree id. Its red therefore comes from the layer witnesses: fsck-dropped is red through the zero-padded case, and entry-types-unchecked through the direct listing.

## The bash-first steer OFF (CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC), 15 Oct 18:22-18:36 EDT, clock read first

The ruling: glitch-6b's study condition, approved by Javier's typed "Your call sounds good". The auto_mode Bash-first steer is OFF for every claude-code take.

### (a) The parser proof

Four probes ran on athena against the pinned take image sha256:336aa8cd... (`docker create --pull=never --network none` + `docker cp -L` + `docker rm`; never run; each work folder deleted after).
Every record reads the binary sha256 24972e3bc859fab2b46ed4c1e51f7d6130f06d3bd550811a114640de3370d0de, which is run_take.CLAUDE_BINARY.

| Step | Binary offset | Excerpt | Record (sha256 of the file) |
|---|---|---|---|
| The gate | 211196350 | `function HXt(){if(a.CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC!==void 0)return a.CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC;switch(ae()){case"forced":return!0;case"none":return!1;case"cohort":return k(G,!1)}}` | claude-2.1.296-thrifty-sonic-hxt-20261015.json (b7132399...) |
| The declaration | export map 207544689; definition 207547480 | `CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC:()=>rE` and `rE=H.triBool()` | claude-2.1.296-thrifty-sonic-getter-20261015.json (d1735de8...) |
| triBool | 207489237 (excerpt sha256 b4e9129b...) | `s=i((n)=>{if(Ne(n))return!0;if(vs(n))return!1;return})`, `triBool:()=>s`, `function i(n){return{parse:(e)=>n(u(e))}}`, `function u(n){return n===void 0?void 0:String(n)}` | claude-2.1.296-tribool-20261015.json (0dd5dd87...) |
| The module's imports | 207488496 (sha256 8ebb3024...) | `import{Ne,vs}from"/$bunfs/root/chunk-j27d47mr.js"` | claude-2.1.296-tribool-helpers-20261015.json (17b4a363...) |
| vs (false) | 207125865 (600-byte sha256 b726117f...) | `function vs(e){if(e===void 0)return!1;if(typeof e==="boolean")return!e;let n=String(e).toLowerCase().trim();return["0","false","no","off"].includes(n)}` | same |
| Ne (true) | 207125723 (600-byte sha256 09daef7f...) | `function Ne(e){if(!e)return!1;if(typeof e==="boolean")return e;let n=String(e).toLowerCase().trim();return["1","true","yes","on"].includes(n)}` | same |

The three candidates against vs: "0", "false" and "off" are all in its list, so all three read FALSE.
None of them is in Ne's list.
HXt then returns false, so the steer is off whatever the cohort says.
**Chosen: "false"** (the spelling ISOLATION_ENV already uses).

Inferred, not proved: that the Ne/vs pair at 207125723 is the chunk-j27d47mr.js the env module imports.
It is the only `function vs(` in the binary with the env-boolean body (the other seven are unrelated), and it sits next to its Ne.
Also inferred: that the env object `a` returns `rE.parse(process.env.CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC)`, from the export map's shape.

### (c) The checker rule: a patch for the harness lane (check_take is harness-owned)

- The box has no transcript reader, and a checker reason id must be routed by routing.json. So the rule lives in check_take, as `lane-reports/stub-take-20261015/proposed-check_take-steer-rule-20261015.patch` (sha256 b0c6f86c...). It applies cleanly to box 0e0bbe20 and to harness 1491e946 (`git apply --check`; nothing applied there).
- `steer_condition_problems(path)` reads every `turn-N.jsonl.launch.json` beside the transcript, and the inline `--settings` env in each.
  - When any turn sets the switch to an off value, an auto_mode attachment record in the transcript is a refusal: `[harness-condition-violated] ... carries N auto_mode attachment(s)`. So is a take whose turns disagree on the switch.
  - The auto_mode records are counted from the raw lines, not through the view, so harness 2c3784e6's furniture admission does not hide them.
  - An unreadable journal or settings value raises CheckRefused.
  - A take that does not set the switch is not this rule's (the product default).
  - check4 and check4_walk add these problems for claude-code.
- The test is `tests/test_steer_condition.py` (in the patch).
  - **Red** on the unpatched check_take (runs/thrifty-06): 5 errors (no steer_condition_problems) and 1 failure (not wired).
  - **Green** patched (runs/thrifty-07): Ran 6, OK, with all four witnesses red.
  - Patched neighbours: test_check_take 15 OK and test_checker_body 29 OK (runs/thrifty-09).
- Replayed over the 25 committed walks (runs/thrifty-08): 0 problems, 0 auto_mode records and 58 journals. None of them sets the switch.
- **Routing (needs a ruling):** `harness-condition-violated` is not in routing.json's operator_reasons. Those are round 3's 24 ids, copied under "gap4 routing: A", and TestRound3Sources re-derives them.
  - As things stand, routing.check_records refuses a take that carries the id as an unlisted checker reason. That fails closed: the take refuses and is never graded.
  - To route it by the generic rows instead (rehearsal before the first turn, harness-error after it), add the id to operator_reasons with a round-4 source entry, and give TestRound3Sources a round-4 exception.
- For the harness lane's battery: checks.d line pins and the COPIED manifest's check_take entry move with the insertion, which sits before `recorded_mode_problems`.

### (d) The departure

The product default under bypassPermissions is model-dependent.
HXt falls back to a server cohort keyed by model: Opus walk w032 got the steer, and the 25 Haiku walks did not.
The study condition is the steer OFF for every claude-code take.
So the agent is never told "While bypass permissions mode is active:" plus the Bash-first steer, which a user of 2.1.296 on Opus would see by default.
This is a departure from the product as shipped. It is chosen so that the arms and models are compared under one instruction set.
It belongs in the draft's harness_furniture_ruling, next to item H.

### Item 3: why the child ended exit=1 (read-only, athena 18:33)

`/home/javier/w-l4F8UdYQ/walk.exit` = 1, written at 15:26:44.
walk.log (3 lines) shows the hold taken by gap4-walk-w032, then `{"results": [{"state": "held", "walk": "w032", "why": "the walk was not committed: ViewError: line 9: unknown attachment type: 'auto_mode'"}], "run_tree": "/tmp/run-7a9eb35f", ...}`. gate.out is `gate: OPEN`.
In walk_runner, `_commit_walk`'s write_walk_grade/commit_walk raised; it returned HELD; walk_builders.cli returns `0 if state == COMMITTED else 1`.
So exit=1 is the w032 hold on the then-unknown auto_mode attachment (now admitted by harness 2c3784e6), not a box or launch failure.
The run tree /tmp/run-7a9eb35f is still on athena (18:38). No w032 folder is listed under the child's repo/evals/gap-study-4/rehearsals/ (it ends at w031); where the held walk's records sit was not read further.
