## Item 2: the isolation flags, findings and glitch-6b's rulings (15 Oct, 18:40-18:58 EDT, clock read)

### Findings

These are from a read-only opus-medium investigation of walk records 197658fe: 58 inits, 25 transcripts and 25 proxy.log files.
No model was asked to try anything, no take was run, and the binary was never run.

| Item | Source | Can it reach outside? | Switch that removes it |
|---|---|---|---|
| Plug-ins cc-plugin-agents-md and cc-plugin-plugin-authoring | Built into the binary. Every init reads `path: builtin`, `…@builtin`. take_config writes `settings.json` = `{}`. | No network path seen | /plugin disable; the enabledPlugins form for @builtin is unverified |
| CronCreate/Delete/List | Binary default | No: in-session, local | CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_CRON=1 (docs) or --disallowedTools |
| **RemoteTrigger** | Binary default. The binary reads `var N$="RemoteTrigger",Mcs="Manage scheduled remote Claude Code agents (routines) via the claude.ai CCR API…"` | **Potentially yes.** It runs claude.ai Routines on the account with the take's own login. Its host is inferred to be api.anthropic.com, the one proxied host. | --disallowedTools RemoteTrigger |
| SendMessage, ListAgents, messaging socket | Binary default | The socket only, and that is local. It binds in the run tree's `.tmp/cc-socks`, a host bind mount, so a host process could connect inward (inferred). | Deny both tools; crossSessionInbound "refuse". The socket still binds; only --bare skips it, and --bare refuses the login. |
| ScheduleWakeup | Binary default (/loop) | No | --disallowedTools |
| Workflow | Binary default | Local subagents only | CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_WORKFLOWS=1 (docs) |
| Skill listing (17 in init; attachment skillCount 14) | Bundled skills plus the built-in plug-in, not an account sync | `schedule` uses RemoteTrigger | CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_BUNDLED_SKILLS=1 (docs) |
| remote_session_change | Binary default: `url` null and attribution strings, identical in all 25 | No | attribution settings (unverified) |

**Proxy logs (230 lines across 25 walks):**
- 205 CONNECT api.anthropic.com:443, tunnel 200.
- 25 CONNECT github.com:443, denied 403.
- All 25 denies, and 25 of the anthropic CONNECTs, come before turn 1. They are preflight's own probes (`preflight.py` OTHER_HOST_VIA_PROXY).
- That leaves 180 agent-era CONNECTs, all to api.anthropic.com.
- Squid logs host:port only, so "inference only" cannot be concluded from the logs.

**Tool calls across all 25 transcripts:** Bash 173 and Read 109, and none of the tools above.

### Rulings (glitch-6b, under the delegation)

**Ruled for every claude-code take:**
- RemoteTrigger, SendMessage and ListAgents are DISALLOWED.
- crossSessionInbound is "refuse".

**Kept, for parity with round 3:**
- Round 3 (2.1.267 and 2.1.280, 64 transcripts) carried the same Cron*, ScheduleWakeup, Workflow and worktree tools, the loop and schedule skills, and remote_session_change.
- So these are kept, with the bundled skills and the built-in plug-ins.
- DISABLE_CRON, DISABLE_WORKFLOWS and DISABLE_BUNDLED_SKILLS are not set.

### Named departures (the harness lane carries the draft wording)

1. **Isolation removals.** RemoteTrigger, SendMessage and ListAgents are removed, and crossSessionInbound is "refuse". Round 3 offered all three tools; no take in either round called one.
2. **The auto_mode bash-first steer is off** (CLAUDE_CODE_THRIFTY_SONIC=false; see the section above).
3. **A version difference.** 2.1.296 lists a new plugin-authoring skill (14 skills against 13). It is kept and named as a version difference.
4. **Residuals:**
   - the messaging socket still binds inside the run tree (a host bind mount);
   - squid logs only CONNECT host:port, so a call to a non-inference path on api.anthropic.com cannot be told apart from inference in the records.

### The build of (1) (box, red-first)

**The names and values come from the binary.** One probe, `probes/claude_string_context_probe.py`, uses docker create --network none plus docker cp and never runs the binary.
- Record `claude-2.1.296-isolation-names-20261015.json` (sha256 ba3e3e6d...):
  - `var N$="RemoteTrigger"` @215378254;
  - `var Gn="SendMessage"` @209532547, with `userFacingName(){return"SendMessage"}`;
  - `var xc="ListAgents"` @215144386, with `userFacingName(){return"ListAgents"}` (the old name ListPeers maps onto ListAgents);
  - `--disallowedTools, --disallowed-tools <tools...>`, "Comma or space-separated list of tool names to deny", @225726398;
  - the policy `case"refuse"` @232960897.
- Record `claude-2.1.296-cross-session-values-20261015.json` (sha256 1ceb44b1...): `var sHt=["accept","hold","refuse"]` @208909790, next to the permissions schema's `deny:`.
- These agree with glitch-6b's relayed reads. An invalid value falls back to "hold", so the exact string is pinned.

**What changed in box/run_take.py:**
- New constants: `ISOLATION_DISALLOWED_TOOLS = ("RemoteTrigger", "SendMessage", "ListAgents")`, `CROSS_SESSION_INBOUND = "refuse"`, and `CLAUDE_DENY_FLAGS`.
- claude_study_settings now carries `crossSessionInbound: "refuse"` and `permissions: {"deny": [the three]}`.
- check_claude_settings refuses a claude-code turn whose argv denies fewer than the three. `_denied_tools` reads the variadic flag (tokens up to the next one starting with "-", or the `=` value), split on commas and spaces.
- The argv is built by claude_code_adapter, which is harness-owned. So `--disallowedTools` gaining the three is a patch: `proposed-claude_code_adapter-isolation-denials-20261015.patch` (sha256 fd9b2209...). It sets `DISALLOWED_TOOLS = ("WebFetch", "WebSearch", *rt.ISOLATION_DISALLOWED_TOOLS)` and updates the adapter test. It applies to box and to harness 1491e946.

**Red and green for test_run_take** (`TestIsolationDenials`, plus the updated CLAUDE_INNER and TestClaudeSettings fixtures, which now carry the deny flag so each negative still tests its own defect, and a `no-deny` negative):
- Red: runs/iso-01, 3 failures.
- Green: runs/iso-02, Ran 64 OK. The six mutants are all red: one tool dropped, a misspelt tool, the argv check dropped, inbound accepted, another binary, and the evidence swapped.

**Coupling: the box head needs the adapter patch.** On the box alone (runs/iso-03):
- test_claude_code_adapter has 13 failures and test_env_allowlist has 3. Both build the argv with the real adapter, which the box now refuses. This is the red the adapter patch closes.
- Every other neighbour module is OK: stub_take, settings_guard, wave4_bindings, preflight, stub_agents, box_integration 61, live_box 38, pending_walk 23, drive_ownership 43 and the two identity modules.
- With the adapter patch applied in a scratch worktree (runs/iso-08): claude_code_adapter 27, env_allowlist 29, run_take 64, stub_take 19, live_box 38 and wave4_bindings 13, all OK.
- **Ship the two together.**

**The checker guard** is a patch for the harness lane, applied after the steer patch: `proposed-check_take-isolation-denials-20261015.patch` (sha256 816e4769...).
- `denied_tool_problems` raises `[harness-condition-violated]` for each turn-stream init that still lists any of the three.
- It is wired into check4 and check4_walk for claude-code, and its test is `tests/test_isolation_denials_checker.py`.
- Red: runs/iso-04, 4 errors and 1 failure.
- Green: runs/iso-05, 5 OK with three witnesses red; the steer test is still 6 OK.
- With it applied, test_check_take is 15 OK and test_checker_body is 29 OK (runs/iso-07).
- Replayed over the 25 committed walks (runs/iso-06): **25 of 25 refuse**. They are pre-ruling walks and offered all three tools.
  - So a walk_regrade recheck of them under this patch would refuse them.
  - The harness lane must scope rechecks to post-ruling takes, or record that refusal as expected.
